Strength Plan

Week 26 targetsSquat 145 kg · Bench 100 kg · Sumo 175 kg

November

Start, then build.

Weeks 1–5 of 26 · 2 Nov – 6 Dec

The plan starts on Monday 2 November.

Week 1 · Start. Record squat depth and choose working weights from recent sets. Test strict pull-ups, dips and push-ups on Thursday.

Week 2 · Build. Squat 4 × 5–6, sumo 3 × 4–5. Progress by completed reps. Flat and paused bench on Tuesday, volume bench and weighted dips on Saturday.

Week 3 · Build. Squat 4 × 5–6, sumo 3 × 4–5. Progress by completed reps. Flat and paused bench on Tuesday, volume bench and weighted dips on Saturday.

Week 4 · Build. Squat 4 × 5–6, sumo 3 × 4–5. Progress by completed reps. Flat and paused bench on Tuesday, volume bench and weighted dips on Saturday.

Week 5 · Build. Squat 4 × 5–6, sumo 3 × 4–5. Progress by completed reps. Flat and paused bench on Tuesday, volume bench and weighted dips on Saturday.

December

Build, then an easier week.

Weeks 6–9 of 26 · 7 Dec – 3 Jan

Week 6 · Build. Squat 4 × 5–6, sumo 3 × 4–5. Progress by completed reps. Flat and paused bench on Tuesday, volume bench and weighted dips on Saturday.

Week 7 · Build. Squat 4 × 5–6, sumo 3 × 4–5. Progress by completed reps. Flat and paused bench on Tuesday, volume bench and weighted dips on Saturday.

Week 8 · Build. Squat 4 × 5–6, sumo 3 × 4–5. Progress by completed reps. Flat and paused bench on Tuesday, volume bench and weighted dips on Saturday.

Week 9 · Easier week. Reduce barbell work and repeat the Thursday bodyweight benchmarks.

January

Strength work and a first practice single for each primary lift.

Weeks 10–13 of 26 · 4 Jan – 31 Jan

Week 10 · Strength. Squat 4 × 3–4 and sumo 3 × 3–4. Keep Tuesday flat and paused bench. Add one easy practice single per primary lift in weeks 12 and 16. Resume Thursday incline from week 8.

Week 11 · Strength. Squat 4 × 3–4 and sumo 3 × 3–4. Keep Tuesday flat and paused bench. Add one easy practice single per primary lift in weeks 12 and 16.

Week 12 · Strength. Before your regular squat, bench and sumo work, take one practice single per lift near RPE 7. Log each separately.

Week 13 · Strength. Squat 4 × 3–4 and sumo 3 × 3–4. Keep Tuesday flat and paused bench. Add one easy practice single per primary lift in weeks 12 and 16.

February

Strength work, a second practice single for each primary lift, then an easier week.

Weeks 14–17 of 26 · 1 Feb – 28 Feb

Week 14 · Strength. Squat 4 × 3–4 and sumo 3 × 3–4. Keep Tuesday flat and paused bench. Add one easy practice single per primary lift in weeks 12 and 16.

Week 15 · Strength. Squat 4 × 3–4 and sumo 3 × 3–4. Keep Tuesday flat and paused bench. Add one easy practice single per primary lift in weeks 12 and 16.

Week 16 · Strength. Before your regular squat, bench and sumo work, take one practice single per lift near RPE 7. Log each separately.

Week 17 · Easier week. Reduce barbell work and repeat the Thursday bodyweight benchmarks.

March

One working single on each main lift, followed by back-off sets.

Weeks 18–22 of 26 · 1 Mar – 4 Apr

Week 18 · Heavy. Each primary lift gets one working single and back-offs. Earn each 5 kg increase on two comparable training weeks. Resume Thursday incline from week 16.

Week 19 · Heavy. Each primary lift gets one working single and back-offs. Earn each 5 kg increase on two comparable training weeks.

Week 20 · Heavy. Each primary lift gets one working single and back-offs. Earn each 5 kg increase on two comparable training weeks.

Week 21 · Heavy. Each primary lift gets one working single and back-offs. Earn each 5 kg increase on two comparable training weeks.

Week 22 · Heavy. Each primary lift gets one working single and back-offs. Earn each 5 kg increase on two comparable training weeks.

April

Heavy practice, opener rehearsal, taper and test week.

Weeks 23–26 of 26 · 5 Apr – 2 May

Week 23 · Heavy. Each primary lift gets one working single and back-offs. Earn each 5 kg increase on two comparable training weeks.

Week 24 · Opener rehearsal. Rehearse familiar openers at RPE 7–8. Reduce back-offs and Thursday incline work.

Week 25 · Taper. Use short, comfortable sets. Main examples: squat 105 kg for 2 × 2, bench 80 kg for 3 × 2, sumo 130 kg for 2 × 2. Thursday incline is one easy set of eight.

Week 26 · Test week. Squat Monday, bench Tuesday, recovery Wednesday, sumo Thursday, recovery Friday and bodyweight tests Saturday. Log results Sunday.

Monday 2 November · Week 1 · Lower A

Squat

About 70 min

1

Mobility warm-up

Before the first lift

  • Easy bike or walk3 min
  • Knee-to-wall ankle rocks2 × 10 per side
  • Adductor rock-backs× 10
  • Goblet squat hold at your depth2 × 20–30 s
  • Hip-hinge drill× 8
2

Before your first squat

Week 1 only

Film from the side with an empty bar and at 60 kg. Find the depth where your hip crease reaches the top of your knee or lower while your lower back stays neutral. If you can't reach it, raise your heels slightly with squat shoes or thin plates. Use that depth for every squat in the plan.

3

Back squat

Main session

Warm-up in kg　20 × 8 · 50 × 5 · 70 × 3 · 85 × 1

  1. Set 1100kg×5
  2. Set 2100kg×5
  3. Set 3100kg×5
  4. Set 4100kg×5

Use the depth you recorded in week 1. Brace before each rep and use rack safeties. Rest 3–5 minutes.

Lighter95 kg × 5, 5, 5, 5Warm-up 20 × 8 · 45 × 5 · 65 × 3 · 80 × 1
Heavier105 kg × 5, 5, 5, 5Warm-up 20 × 8 · 50 × 5 · 70 × 3 · 90 × 1

Use the first main session to check the starting load at your usual technique. If all sets are clean with at least four reps left on the last, the next main session can start 5 kg higher. If the work is hard, repeat the load or use the lighter example.

4

Paused sumo

Second session

Warm-up  60 × 5 · 70 × 2

  1. Set 190kg×3
  2. Set 290kg×3

Two easy technique sets. Pause for one second just off the floor, reset every rep and leave at least three clean reps in reserve. Rest 2–3 minutes. Use an easier row or one set if Monday fatigue carries into the main sumo session. These sets do not earn load increases.

Lighter80 kg × 3, 3Warm-up 60 × 5
Heavier95 kg × 3, 3Warm-up 60 × 5 · 75 × 2
5

Accessories

Choose the weight by feel

  • Leg extension2 × 10–151 left · rest 90 s. Pad just above the ankle, knee in line with the machine pivot.
  • Seated leg curl2 × 8–121 left · rest 90 s. Hips back in the seat; brief squeeze with the knees bent. Use the lying leg curl only if this one is taken; the seated one grows the hamstrings more.
  • Standing calf raise2 × 8–121 left · rest 60–90 s. Smith machine or leg-press calf raise; pause 1–2 s in the bottom stretch.
6

Stretch

6–8 minutes, after the session

  • Lying hamstring strap stretch2 × 45 s per side
  • Half-kneeling hip flexor2 × 30 s per side
  • Frog inner-thigh stretch2 × 45 s
  • Wall calf stretch2 × 30 s per side

Tuesday 3 November · Week 1 · Upper A

Heavy bench

About 65 min

1

Bench press

Main session

Warm-up  20 × 8 · 30 × 5 · 45 × 3 · 55 × 1

  1. Set 170kg×6
  2. Set 270kg×6
  3. Set 370kg×5
  4. Then paused bench
  5. Paused 165kg×4
  6. Paused 265kg×4

Rest 3–5 minutes. Use the same grip and touch, a spotter or safeties, and a one-second pause on paused reps. If all flat and paused work is clean and four or more reps remain on the final flat set, you can move one 5 kg step next Tuesday (70 → 75 kg from Normal). Select 80 kg only when your recent work already supports it.

Lighter65 kg × 6, 6, 5, then paused 2 × 4 @ 60Warm-up 20 × 8 · 30 × 5 · 40 × 3 · 55 × 1
Heavier75 kg × 6, 6, 5, then paused 2 × 4 @ 70Warm-up 20 × 8 · 35 × 5 · 50 × 3 · 60 × 1

Bench goal. Start from your reported 90 kg single and 80 kg × 4–5. Work toward a clean 100 kg single. 105 kg is a stretch if late training supports it. Log the grip, touch standard, load, reps and final unpaused-set effort.

2

Weighted pull-up

Main session

Warm-up  BW × 5 · +5 × 3

  1. Set 1+12.5kg×4
  2. Set 2+12.5kg×4
  3. Set 3+12.5kg×4
  4. Set 4+12.5kg×4

Rest 3 min. Dead hang to chin over the bar, no swing. First week: if the last set has 4 or more reps left, use the Heavier row from the next session.

Lighter+10 kg × 4, 4, 4, 4Warm-up BW × 5 · +5 × 3
Heavier+15 kg × 4, 4, 4, 4Warm-up BW × 5 · +7.5 × 3
3

Accessories

Choose the weight by feel

  • Chest-supported dumbbell row2 × 8–121–2 left · rest 2 min. Chest on an incline bench; pull the elbows toward your hips. A row machine with a chest pad works too.
  • Cable lateral raise2 × 12–201 left · rest 60–90 s. Raise to shoulder height without swinging. The standing multi-flight machine works just as well.
  • Hanging knee raise2 × 8–151–2 left · rest 90 s. Curl the pelvis up slightly at the top; no swinging.
4

Stretch

6–8 minutes, after the session

  • Lying hamstring strap stretch2 × 45 s per side
  • 90/90 hip switches× 8
  • Figure-4 glute stretch2 × 30 s per side

Wednesday 4 November · Week 1 · Lower B

Sumo

About 65 min

1

Mobility warm-up

Before the first lift

  • Easy bike or walk3 min
  • Knee-to-wall ankle rocks2 × 10 per side
  • Adductor rock-backs× 10
  • Goblet squat hold at your depth2 × 20–30 s
  • Hip-hinge drill× 8
2

Sumo deadlift

Main session

Warm-up in kg　60 × 5 · 80 × 3 · 100 × 1

  1. Set 1120kg×4
  2. Set 2120kg×4
  3. Set 3120kg×4

Start each rep from a full reset. Brace, keep the bar close and use your usual lockout. Rest 3–5 minutes.

Lighter115 kg × 4, 4, 4Warm-up 60 × 5 · 80 × 3 · 95 × 1
Heavier125 kg × 4, 4, 4Warm-up 60 × 5 · 85 × 3 · 105 × 1

Use the first main session to check the starting load at your usual technique. If all sets are clean with at least four reps left on the last, the next main session can start 5 kg higher. If the work is hard, repeat the load or use the lighter example.

3

Accessories

Choose the weight by feel

  • Bulgarian split squat2 × 8–12 per leg2 left · rest 2 min. Rear foot on a bench, dumbbells at your sides; straps if grip fades.
  • Leg press2 × 10–151–2 left · rest 2 min. Lower until just before your pelvis starts to roll off the pad. If it's taken, use the hack squat for the same reps, with your heels down and your lower back on the pad.
  • Seated calf raise machine2 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Pause 1–2 s in the bottom stretch; no bouncing.
  • Hip abduction machine2 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Sit tall, push your knees out, pause 1 s with your legs wide, then return slowly.
  • Hip adduction machine2 × 10–151–2 left · rest 60–90 s. Start as wide as is comfortable, squeeze the pads together, then open slowly without bouncing.
4

Stretch

6–8 minutes, after the session

  • Lying hamstring strap stretch2 × 45 s per side
  • Half-kneeling hip flexor2 × 30 s per side
  • Frog inner-thigh stretch2 × 45 s
  • Wall calf stretch2 × 30 s per side

Thursday 5 November · Week 1 · Upper B

Bodyweight benchmarks

About 55 min

1

Bodyweight tests

In this order, 5 minutes apart

  1. Pull-upsDead hang, chin over the barMax reps
  2. DipsYour usual depth, full lockoutMax reps
  3. Push-upsChest to a fist-height markerMax reps

Log clean reps and bodyweight. Keep the same test order at each benchmark. These single-set tests take the place of Thursday’s incline work.

2

Accessories

Choose the weight by feel

  • Reverse pec deck2 × 12–201 left · rest 60–90 s. Open the arms without shrugging.
  • Incline or preacher curl2 × 8–121 left · rest 90 s. Incline with dumbbells, or preacher on the bench or the cable preacher. Upper arm still; lower under control.
  • Pallof press2 × 10–12 per siderest 60 s. Stand side-on to the cable, press straight out and resist turning.
  • Wrist curl2 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Forearms on a bench or the forearm cable machine, palms up; let the handle roll down to your fingers, then curl your wrists up.
  • Reverse wrist curl2 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Straight after the wrist curl, palms down; lift the backs of your hands, then lower slowly.
3

Stretch

6–8 minutes, after the session

  • Lying hamstring strap stretch2 × 45 s per side
  • 90/90 hip switches× 8
  • Figure-4 glute stretch2 × 30 s per side

Friday 6 November · Week 1 · Lower C

Lighter squat day

About 60 min

1

Mobility warm-up

Before the first lift

  • Easy bike or walk3 min
  • Knee-to-wall ankle rocks2 × 10 per side
  • Adductor rock-backs× 10
  • Goblet squat hold at your depth2 × 20–30 s
  • Hip-hinge drill× 8
2

Paused squat

Second session

Warm-up  20 × 8 · 50 × 5 · 65 × 2

  1. Set 180kg×4
  2. Set 280kg×4
  3. Set 380kg×4

2-second pause at your depth, stay tight, no bounce. Rest 3 min. Uses the same row as this lift's main session.

Lighter70 kg × 4, 4, 4Warm-up 20 × 8 · 40 × 5
Heavier90 kg × 4, 4, 4Warm-up 20 × 8 · 55 × 5 · 70 × 2
3

Accessories

Choose the weight by feel

  • 45° back extension2 × 10–152 left · rest 90 s. Pad just below the hip crease; hinge at the hips with a neutral back.
  • Seated leg curl2 × 10–151 left · rest 90 s. Same seat and pad settings as Monday.
  • Standing calf raise2 × 10–151 left · rest 60–90 s. Pause 1–2 s in the bottom stretch; no bouncing.
  • Cable crunch2 × 10–151–2 left · rest 60 s. Kneel facing a high pulley with the rope beside your head; curl your ribs toward your hips without sitting back.
  • Side plank2 × 20–40 s per siderest 60 s. Shoulder, hip and ankle in line; bend the knees if needed.
4

Stretch

6–8 minutes, after the session

  • Lying hamstring strap stretch2 × 45 s per side
  • Half-kneeling hip flexor2 × 30 s per side
  • Frog inner-thigh stretch2 × 45 s
  • Wall calf stretch2 × 30 s per side

Weighted dip

Main session

  1. Set 1+17.5kg×6

Lighter+15 kg × 6, 5, 5Warm-up BW × 5 · +7.5 × 3
Heavier+20 kg × 6, 5, 5Warm-up BW × 5 · +10 × 3
3

Accessories

Choose the weight by feel

  • Overhead cable triceps extension2 × 10–151 left · rest 90 s. Rope on a low pulley, face away and hold it behind your head; let your elbows bend fully, then straighten your arms.
  • Hammer curl2 × 10–151 left · rest 60–90 s. Thumbs up; no swinging.

Back squat

Main session

Warm-up in kg　20 × 8 · 50 × 5 · 70 × 3 · 85 × 1

  1. Set 1100kg×6
  2. Set 2100kg×6
  3. Set 3100kg×6
  4. Set 4100kg×6

Use the depth you recorded in week 1. Brace before each rep and use rack safeties. Rest 3–5 minutes.

Lighter95 kg × 6, 6, 6, 6Warm-up 20 × 8 · 45 × 5 · 65 × 3 · 80 × 1
Heavier105 kg × 6, 6, 6, 6Warm-up 20 × 8 · 50 × 5 · 70 × 3 · 90 × 1

Work range 4 × 5–6. Use the row your log supports. Start a new weight at the bottom of the rep range. Move up 5 kg on a future main day only after two comparable sessions at the same weight reach the top on every set, with at least two clean reps left on the final set both times. With one left, repeat. After a miss or a grind, use the next lighter load. Easier weeks and secondary work do not count.

All load options and warm-ups
  • 95 kg for 4 × 5–6. Warm-up in kg: 20 × 8 · 45 × 5 · 65 × 3 · 80 × 1.
  • 100 kg for 4 × 5–6. Warm-up in kg: 20 × 8 · 50 × 5 · 70 × 3 · 85 × 1.
  • 105 kg for 4 × 5–6. Warm-up in kg: 20 × 8 · 50 × 5 · 70 × 3 · 90 × 1.
  • 110 kg for 4 × 5–6. Warm-up in kg: 20 × 8 · 55 × 5 · 75 × 3 · 90 × 1.
  • 115 kg for 4 × 5–6. Warm-up in kg: 20 × 8 · 55 × 5 · 80 × 3 · 95 × 1.

Tuesday 10 November · Week 2 · Upper A

Heavy bench

About 65 min

1

Bench press

Main session

Warm-up  20 × 8 · 35 × 5 · 50 × 3 · 60 × 1

  1. Set 175kg×5
  2. Set 275kg×5
  3. Set 375kg×4
  4. Then paused bench
  5. Paused 170kg×4
  6. Paused 270kg×4

Rest 3–5 min. Touch the chest with no bounce; safeties set or a spotter. Paused sets: 1-second pause on the chest, rest 3 min.

Lighter70 kg × 5, 5, 4, then paused 2 × 4 @ 65Warm-up 20 × 8 · 30 × 5 · 45 × 3 · 55 × 1
Heavier80 kg × 5, 5, 4, then paused 2 × 4 @ 75Warm-up 20 × 8 · 35 × 5 · 50 × 3 · 65 × 1

Choose next week’s row. After two regular Tuesdays in the same row with all flat and paused reps complete and three or more left on the last unpaused set, move up one row next regular Tuesday. If already on Heavier, add one rep on the first flat set, up to 8, leaving at least two in hand. A missed rep or all-out final set calls for one row less.

Thursday 12 November · Week 2 · Upper B

Pull-ups, strict push-ups and incline bench

About 55 min

1

Pull-ups

Printed reps, then one set to 1 left

  1. Set 115reps
  2. Set 212reps
  3. Set 3Max, stop with 1 left

Rest 3 min between sets. If you reach 1 rep left before a printed number, stop there.

2

Strict push-ups

Printed reps, then one set to 1 left

  1. Set 135reps
  2. Set 2Max, stop with 1 left

Rest 3 min between sets. If you reach 1 rep left before a printed number, stop there.

3

Low-incline bench press

Two work sets · 2 clean reps left

Use a 30° bench. Warm up with 20 kg × 8 for working weights of 25 kg or more. Add 30 kg × 5 when working at 35 kg or above.

  1. Set 18–12reps
  2. Set 28–12reps

Working weight. First session, try 45 kg total. Use 40 kg if eight clean reps at 45 kg would leave fewer than two in reserve. On later Thursdays, use your last regular working weight. Once both sets reach 12 at the same load with at least two clean reps left, use the next 5 kg step at the next regular session and aim for eight per set.

Barbell steps and setup

20 · 25 · 30 · 35 · 40 · 45 · 50 · 55 · 60 · 65 · 70 kg. Continue in 5 kg steps. All weights include the bar. For work at 50 kg or above, an extra 1–3 warm-up reps one step below your working weight can help you judge today’s load.

Use safeties or a spotter. Keep your feet planted and your back comfortably supported. Use the same bench setting and controlled range each week. Rest 2–3 minutes between work sets.

If a new weight works for the first eight but is too heavy for the second set, use the previous weight for set two. Log both loads and both rep counts.

4

Accessories

Choose the weight by feel

  • Reverse pec deck3 × 12–201 left · rest 60–90 s. Open the arms without shrugging.
  • Incline or preacher curl3 × 8–121 left · rest 90 s. Incline with dumbbells, or preacher on the bench or the cable preacher. Upper arm still; lower under control.
  • Pallof press2 × 10–12 per siderest 60 s. Stand side-on to the cable, press straight out and resist turning.
  • Wrist curl2 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Forearms on a bench or the forearm cable machine, palms up; let the handle roll down to your fingers, then curl your wrists up.
  • Reverse wrist curl2 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Straight after the wrist curl, palms down; lift the backs of your hands, then lower slowly.
5

Stretch

Accessories

Choose the weight by feel

Monday 16 November · Week 3 · Lower A

Squat

About 70 min

1

Mobility warm-up

Before the first lift

  • Easy bike or walk3 min
  • Knee-to-wall ankle rocks2 × 10 per side
  • Adductor rock-backs× 10
  • Goblet squat hold at your depth2 × 20–30 s
  • Hip-hinge drill× 8
2

Back squat

Main session

Warm-up in kg　20 × 8 · 50 × 5 · 70 × 3 · 85 × 1

  1. Set 1100kg×6
  2. Set 2100kg×6
  3. Set 3100kg×6
  4. Set 4100kg×6

Use the depth you recorded in week 1. Brace before each rep and use rack safeties. Rest 3–5 minutes.

Lighter95 kg × 6, 6, 6, 6Warm-up 20 × 8 · 45 × 5 · 65 × 3 · 80 × 1
Heavier105 kg × 6, 6, 6, 6Warm-up 20 × 8 · 50 × 5 · 70 × 3 · 90 × 1

Work range 4 × 5–6. Use the row your log supports. Start a new weight at the bottom of the rep range. Move up 5 kg on a future main day only after two comparable sessions at the same weight reach the top on every set, with at least two clean reps left on the final set both times. With one left, repeat. After a miss or a grind, use the next lighter load. Easier weeks and secondary work do not count.

All load options and warm-ups
  • 95 kg for 4 × 5–6. Warm-up in kg: 20 × 8 · 45 × 5 · 65 × 3 · 80 × 1.
  • 100 kg for 4 × 5–6. Warm-up in kg: 20 × 8 · 50 × 5 · 70 × 3 · 85 × 1.
  • 105 kg for 4 × 5–6. Warm-up in kg: 20 × 8 · 50 × 5 · 70 × 3 · 90 × 1.
  • 110 kg for 4 × 5–6. Warm-up in kg: 20 × 8 · 55 × 5 · 75 × 3 · 90 × 1.
  • 115 kg for 4 × 5–6. Warm-up in kg: 20 × 8 · 55 × 5 · 80 × 3 · 95 × 1.
3

Paused sumo

Second session

Warm-up  60 × 5 · 80 × 2

  1. Set 1100kg×3
  2. Set 2100kg×3

Two easy technique sets. Pause for one second just off the floor, reset every rep and leave at least three clean reps in reserve. Rest 2–3 minutes. Use an easier row or one set if Monday fatigue carries into the main sumo session. These sets do not earn load increases.

Lighter90 kg × 3, 3Warm-up 60 × 5 · 70 × 2
Heavier110 kg × 3, 3Warm-up 60 × 5 · 90 × 2
4

Accessories

Choose the weight by feel

  • Leg extension3 × 10–151 left · rest 90 s. Pad just above the ankle, knee in line with the machine pivot.
  • Seated leg curl3 × 8–121 left · rest 90 s. Hips back in the seat; brief squeeze with the knees bent. Use the lying leg curl only if this one is taken; the seated one grows the hamstrings more.
  • Standing calf raise4 × 8–121 left · rest 60–90 s. Smith machine or leg-press calf raise; pause 1–2 s in the bottom stretch.
5

Stretch

6–8 minutes, after the session

  • Lying hamstring strap stretch2 × 45 s per side
  • Half-kneeling hip flexor2 × 30 s per side
  • Frog inner-thigh stretch2 × 45 s
  • Wall calf stretch2 × 30 s per side

Tuesday 17 November · Week 3 · Upper A

Heavy bench

About 65 min

1

Bench press

Main session

Warm-up  20 × 8 · 35 × 5 · 50 × 3 · 60 × 1

  1. Set 175kg×5
  2. Set 275kg×5
  3. Set 375kg×4
  4. Then paused bench
  5. Paused 170kg×4
  6. Paused 270kg×4

Rest 3–5 min. Touch the chest with no bounce; safeties set or a spotter. Paused sets: 1-second pause on the chest, rest 3 min.

Lighter70 kg × 5, 5, 4, then paused 2 × 4 @ 65Warm-up 20 × 8 · 30 × 5 · 45 × 3 · 55 × 1
Heavier80 kg × 5, 5, 4, then paused 2 × 4 @ 75Warm-up 20 × 8 · 35 × 5 · 50 × 3 · 65 × 1

Choose next week’s row. After two regular Tuesdays in the same row with all flat and paused reps complete and three or more left on the last unpaused set, move up one row next regular Tuesday. If already on Heavier, add one rep on the first flat set, up to 8, leaving at least two in hand. A missed rep or all-out final set calls for one row less.

2

Weighted pull-up

Main session

Warm-up  BW × 5 · +7.5 × 3

  1. Set 1+15kg×4
  2. Set 2+15kg×4
  3. Set 3+15kg×4
  4. Set 4+15kg×4

Rest 3 min. Dead hang to chin over the bar, no swing.

Lighter+12.5 kg × 4, 4, 4, 4Warm-up BW × 5 · +5 × 3
Heavier+17.5 kg × 4, 4, 4, 4Warm-up BW × 5 · +7.5 × 3
3

Accessories

Choose the weight by feel

  • Chest-supported dumbbell row3 × 8–121–2 left · rest 2 min. Chest on an incline bench; pull the elbows toward your hips. A row machine with a chest pad works too.
  • Cable lateral raise3 × 12–201 left · rest 60–90 s. Raise to shoulder height without swinging. The standing multi-flight machine works just as well.
  • Hanging knee raise2 × 8–151–2 left · rest 90 s. Curl the pelvis up slightly at the top; no swinging.
4

Stretch

6–8 minutes, after the session

  • Lying hamstring strap stretch2 × 45 s per side
  • 90/90 hip switches× 8
  • Figure-4 glute stretch2 × 30 s per side

Wednesday 18 November · Week 3 · Lower B

Sumo

  • Knee-to-wall ankle rocks2 × 10 per side
  • Adductor rock-backs× 10
  • Goblet squat hold at your depth2 × 20–30 s
  • Hip-hinge drill× 8
2

Sumo deadlift

Main session

Warm-up in kg　60 × 5 · 80 × 3 · 100 × 1

  1. Set 1120kg×5
  2. Set 2120kg×5
  3. Set 3120kg×5

Start each rep from a full reset. Brace, keep the bar close and use your usual lockout. Rest 3–5 minutes.

Lighter115 kg × 5, 5, 5Warm-up 60 × 5 · 80 × 3 · 95 × 1
Heavier125 kg × 5, 5, 5Warm-up 60 × 5 · 85 × 3 · 105 × 1

Work range 3 × 4–5. Use the row your log supports. Start a new weight at the bottom of the rep range. Move up 5 kg on a future main day only after two comparable sessions at the same weight reach the top on every set, with at least two clean reps left on the final set both times. With one left, repeat. After a miss or a grind, use the next lighter load. Easier weeks and secondary work do not count.

All load options and warm-ups
  • 115 kg for 3 × 4–5. Warm-up in kg: 60 × 5 · 80 × 3 · 95 × 1.
  • 120 kg for 3 × 4–5. Warm-up in kg: 60 × 5 · 80 × 3 · 100 × 1.
  • 125 kg for 3 × 4–5. Warm-up in kg: 60 × 5 · 85 × 3 · 105 × 1.
  • 130 kg for 3 × 4–5. Warm-up in kg: 60 × 5 · 90 × 3 · 110 × 1.
  • 135 kg for 3 × 4–5. Warm-up in kg: 60 × 5 · 65 × 5 · 90 × 3 · 115 × 1.
3

Accessories

Choose the weight by feel

  • Bulgarian split squat3 × 8–12 per leg2 left · rest 2 min. Rear foot on a bench, dumbbells at your sides; straps if grip fades.
  • Leg press3 × 10–151–2 left · rest 2 min. Lower until just before your pelvis starts to roll off the pad. If it's taken, use the hack squat for the same reps, with your heels down and your lower back on the pad.
  • Seated calf raise machine3 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Pause 1–2 s in the bottom stretch; no bouncing.
  • Hip abduction machine3 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Sit tall, push your knees out, pause 1 s with your legs wide, then return slowly.
  • Hip adduction machine2 × 10–151–2 left · rest 60–90 s. Start as wide as is comfortable, squeeze the pads together, then open slowly without bouncing.
4

Stretch

6–8 minutes, after the session

  • Lying hamstring strap stretch2 × 45 s per side
  • Half-kneeling hip flexor2 × 30 s per side
  • Frog inner-thigh stretch2 × 45 s
  • Wall calf stretch2 × 30 s per side

Thursday 19 November · Week 3 · Upper B

Pull-ups, strict push-ups and incline bench

About 55 min

1

Pull-ups

Printed reps, then one set to 1 left

  1. Set 115reps
  2. Set 212reps
  3. Set 3Max, stop with 1 left

Rest 3 min between sets. If you reach 1 rep left before a printed number, stop there.

2

Strict push-ups

Printed reps, then one set to 1 left

  1. Set 135reps
  2. Set 2Max, stop with 1 left

Rest 3 min between sets. If you reach 1 rep left before a printed number, stop there.

3

Low-incline bench press

Two work sets · 2 clean reps left

Use a 30° bench. Warm up with 20 kg × 8 for working weights of 25 kg or more. Add 30 kg × 5 when working at 35 kg or above.

  1. Set 18–12reps
  2. Set 28–12reps

Working weight. First session, try 45 kg total. Use 40 kg if eight clean reps at 45 kg would leave fewer than two in reserve. On later Thursdays, use your last regular working weight. Once both sets reach 12 at the same load with at least two clean reps left, use the next 5 kg step at the next regular session and aim for eight per set.

Barbell steps and setup

20 · 25 · 30 · 35 · 40 · 45 · 50 · 55 · 60 · 65 · 70 kg. Continue in 5 kg steps. All weights include the bar. For work at 50 kg or above, an extra 1–3 warm-up reps one step below your working weight can help you judge today’s load.

Use safeties or a spotter. Keep your feet planted and your back comfortably supported. Use the same bench setting and controlled range each week. Rest 2–3 minutes between work sets.

If a new weight works for the first eight but is too heavy for the second set, use the previous weight for set two. Log both loads and both rep counts.

4

Accessories

Choose the weight by feel

  • Reverse pec deck3 × 12–201 left · rest 60–90 s. Open the arms without shrugging.
  • Incline or preacher curl3 × 8–121 left · rest 90 s. Incline with dumbbells, or preacher on the bench or the cable preacher. Upper arm still; lower under control.
  • Pallof press2 × 10–12 per siderest 60 s. Stand side-on to the cable, press straight out and resist turning.
  • Wrist curl2 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Forearms on a bench or the forearm cable machine, palms up; let the handle roll down to your fingers, then curl your wrists up.
  • Reverse wrist curl2 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Straight after the wrist curl, palms down; lift the backs of your hands, then lower slowly.
5

Stretch

6–8 minutes, after the session

  • Lying hamstring strap stretch2 × 45 s per side
  • 90/90 hip switches× 8
  • Figure-4 glute stretch2 × 30 s per side

Rest 3 min between sets. If you reach 1 rep left before a printed number, stop there.

Strict push-ups

Printed reps, then one set to 1 left

  1. Set 136reps
  2. Set 2Max, stop with 1 left

Rest 3 min between sets. If you reach 1 rep left before a printed number, stop there.

3

Low-incline bench press

Two work sets · 2 clean reps left

Use a 30° bench. Warm up with 20 kg × 8 for working weights of 25 kg or more. Add 30 kg × 5 when working at 35 kg or above.

  1. Set 18–12reps
  2. Set 28–12reps

Working weight. First session, try 45 kg total. Use 40 kg if eight clean reps at 45 kg would leave fewer than two in reserve. On later Thursdays, use your last regular working weight. Once both sets reach 12 at the same load with at least two clean reps left, use the next 5 kg step at the next regular session and aim for eight per set.

Barbell steps and setup

20 · 25 · 30 · 35 · 40 · 45 · 50 · 55 · 60 · 65 · 70 kg. Continue in 5 kg steps. All weights include the bar. For work at 50 kg or above, an extra 1–3 warm-up reps one step below your working weight can help you judge today’s load.

Use safeties or a spotter. Keep your feet planted and your back comfortably supported. Use the same bench setting and controlled range each week. Rest 2–3 minutes between work sets.

If a new weight works for the first eight but is too heavy for the second set, use the previous weight for set two. Log both loads and both rep counts.

4

Accessories

Choose the weight by feel

  • Reverse pec deck3 × 12–201 left · rest 60–90 s. Open the arms without shrugging.
  • Incline or preacher curl3 × 8–121 left · rest 90 s. Incline with dumbbells, or preacher on the bench or the cable preacher. Upper arm still; lower under control.
  • Pallof press2 × 10–12 per siderest 60 s. Stand side-on to the cable, press straight out and resist turning.
  • Wrist curl2 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Forearms on a bench or the forearm cable machine, palms up; let the handle roll down to your fingers, then curl your wrists up.
  • Reverse wrist curl2 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Straight after the wrist curl, palms down; lift the backs of your hands, then lower slowly.
5

Stretch

6–8 minutes, after the session

  • Lying hamstring strap stretch2 × 45 s per side
  • 90/90 hip switches× 8
  • Figure-4 glute stretch2 × 30 s per side

About 70 min

Mobility warm-up

Before the first lift

  • Knee-to-wall ankle rocks2 × 10 per side
  • Adductor rock-backs× 10
  • Goblet squat hold at your depth2 × 20–30 s
  • Hip-hinge drill× 8
2

Back squat

Main session

Warm-up in kg　20 × 8 · 50 × 5 · 70 × 3 · 90 × 1

  1. Set 1105kg×6
  2. Set 2105kg×6
  3. Set 3105kg×6
  4. Set 4105kg×6

Use the depth you recorded in week 1. Brace before each rep and use rack safeties. Rest 3–5 minutes.

Lighter100 kg × 6, 6, 6, 6Warm-up 20 × 8 · 50 × 5 · 70 × 3 · 85 × 1
Heavier110 kg × 6, 6, 6, 6Warm-up 20 × 8 · 55 × 5 · 75 × 3 · 90 × 1

Work range 4 × 5–6. Use the row your log supports. Start a new weight at the bottom of the rep range. Move up 5 kg on a future main day only after two comparable sessions at the same weight reach the top on every set, with at least two clean reps left on the final set both times. With one left, repeat. After a miss or a grind, use the next lighter load. Easier weeks and secondary work do not count.

All load options and warm-ups
  • 95 kg for 4 × 5–6. Warm-up in kg: 20 × 8 · 45 × 5 · 65 × 3 · 80 × 1.
  • 100 kg for 4 × 5–6. Warm-up in kg: 20 × 8 · 50 × 5 · 70 × 3 · 85 × 1.
  • 105 kg for 4 × 5–6. Warm-up in kg: 20 × 8 · 50 × 5 · 70 × 3 · 90 × 1.
  • 110 kg for 4 × 5–6. Warm-up in kg: 20 × 8 · 55 × 5 · 75 × 3 · 90 × 1.
  • 115 kg for 4 × 5–6. Warm-up in kg: 20 × 8 · 55 × 5 · 80 × 3 · 95 × 1.
3

Paused sumo

Second session

Warm-up  60 × 5 · 80 × 2

  1. Set 1100kg×3
  2. Set 2100kg×3

Two easy technique sets. Pause for one second just off the floor, reset every rep and leave at least three clean reps in reserve. Rest 2–3 minutes. Use an easier row or one set if Monday fatigue carries into the main sumo session. These sets do not earn load increases.

Lighter90 kg × 3, 3Warm-up 60 × 5 · 70 × 2
Heavier110 kg × 3, 3Warm-up 60 × 5 · 90 × 2
4

Accessories

Choose the weight by feel

  • Leg extension3 × 10–151 left · rest 90 s. Pad just above the ankle, knee in line with the machine pivot.
  • Seated leg curl3 × 8–121 left · rest 90 s. Hips back in the seat; brief squeeze with the knees bent. Use the lying leg curl only if this one is taken; the seated one grows the hamstrings more.
  • Standing calf raise4 × 8–121 left · rest 60–90 s. Smith machine or leg-press calf raise; pause 1–2 s in the bottom stretch.
5

Stretch

6–8 minutes, after the session

  • Lying hamstring strap stretch2 × 45 s per side
  • Half-kneeling hip flexor2 × 30 s per side
  • Frog inner-thigh stretch2 × 45 s
  • Wall calf stretch2 × 30 s per side

Tuesday 1 December · Week 5 · Upper A

Heavy bench

About 65 min

1

Bench press

Main session

Warm-up  20 × 8 · 35 × 5 · 50 × 3 · 60 × 1

  1. Set 175kg×5
  2. Set 275kg×5
  3. Set 375kg×5
  4. Then paused bench
  5. Paused 170kg×4
  6. Paused 270kg×4

Rest 3–5 min. Touch the chest with no bounce; safeties set or a spotter. Paused sets: 1-second pause on the chest, rest 3 min.

Lighter70 kg × 5, 5, 5, then paused 2 × 4 @ 65Warm-up 20 × 8 · 30 × 5 · 45 × 3 · 55 × 1
Heavier80 kg × 5, 5, 5, then paused 2 × 4 @ 75Warm-up 20 × 8 · 35 × 5 · 50 × 3 · 65 × 1

Choose next week’s row. After two regular Tuesdays in the same row with all flat and paused reps complete and three or more left on the last unpaused set, move up one row next regular Tuesday. If already on Heavier, add one rep on the first flat set, up to 8, leaving at least two in hand. A missed rep or all-out final set calls for one row less.

3

Accessories

Choose the weight by feel

Accessories

Choose the weight by feel

  • Bulgarian split squat3 × 8–12 per leg2 left · rest 2 min. Rear foot on a bench, dumbbells at your sides; straps if grip fades.
  • Leg press3 × 10–151–2 left · rest 2 min. Lower until just before your pelvis starts to roll off the pad. If it's taken, use the hack squat for the same reps, with your heels down and your lower back on the pad.
  • Seated calf raise machine3 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Pause 1–2 s in the bottom stretch; no bouncing.
  • Hip abduction machine3 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Sit tall, push your knees out, pause 1 s with your legs wide, then return slowly.
  • Hip adduction machine2 × 10–151–2 left · rest 60–90 s. Start as wide as is comfortable, squeeze the pads together, then open slowly without bouncing.

With one left, repeat. After a miss or a grind, use the next lighter load. Easier weeks and secondary work do not count.

All load options and warm-ups
  • 115 kg for 3 × 4–5. Warm-up in kg: 60 × 5 · 80 × 3 · 95 × 1.
  • 120 kg for 3 × 4–5. Warm-up in kg: 60 × 5 · 80 × 3 · 100 × 1.
  • 125 kg for 3 × 4–5. Warm-up in kg: 60 × 5 · 85 × 3 · 105 × 1.
  • 130 kg for 3 × 4–5. Warm-up in kg: 60 × 5 · 90 × 3 · 110 × 1.
  • 135 kg for 3 × 4–5. Warm-up in kg: 60 × 5 · 65 × 5 · 90 × 3 · 115 × 1.
3

Accessories

Choose the weight by feel

  • Bulgarian split squat3 × 8–12 per leg2 left · rest 2 min. Rear foot on a bench, dumbbells at your sides; straps if grip fades.
  • Leg press3 × 10–151–2 left · rest 2 min. Lower until just before your pelvis starts to roll off the pad. If it's taken, use the hack squat for the same reps, with your heels down and your lower back on the pad.
  • Seated calf raise machine3 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Pause 1–2 s in the bottom stretch; no bouncing.
  • Hip abduction machine3 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Sit tall, push your knees out, pause 1 s with your legs wide, then return slowly.
  • Hip adduction machine2 × 10–151–2 left · rest 60–90 s. Start as wide as is comfortable, squeeze the pads together, then open slowly without bouncing.
4

Stretch

6–8 minutes, after the session

  • Lying hamstring strap stretch2 × 45 s per side
  • Half-kneeling hip flexor2 × 30 s per side
  • Frog inner-thigh stretch2 × 45 s
  • Wall calf stretch2 × 30 s per side

Thursday 10 December · Week 6 · Upper B

Pull-ups, strict push-ups and incline bench

About 55 min

1

Pull-ups

Printed reps, then one set to 1 left

  1. Set 116reps
  2. Set 213reps
  3. Set 3Max, stop with 1 left

Rest 3 min between sets. If you reach 1 rep left before a printed number, stop there.

2

Strict push-ups

Printed reps, then one set to 1 left

  1. Set 137reps
  2. Set 2Max, stop with 1 left

Rest 3 min between sets. If you reach 1 rep left before a printed number, stop there.

3

Low-incline bench press

Two work sets · 2 clean reps left

Use a 30° bench. Warm up with 20 kg × 8 for working weights of 25 kg or more. Add 30 kg × 5 when working at 35 kg or above.

  1. Set 18–12reps
  2. Set 28–12reps

Working weight. First session, try 45 kg total. Use 40 kg if eight clean reps at 45 kg would leave fewer than two in reserve. On later Thursdays, use your last regular working weight. Once both sets reach 12 at the same load with at least two clean reps left, use the next 5 kg step at the next regular session and aim for eight per set.

Barbell steps and setup

20 · 25 · 30 · 35 · 40 · 45 · 50 · 55 · 60 · 65 · 70 kg. Continue in 5 kg steps. All weights include the bar. For work at 50 kg or above, an extra 1–3 warm-up reps one step below your working weight can help you judge today’s load.

Use safeties or a spotter. Keep your feet planted and your back comfortably supported. Use the same bench setting and controlled range each week. Rest 2–3 minutes between work sets.

If a new weight works for the first eight but is too heavy for the second set, use the previous weight for set two. Log both loads and both rep counts.

4

Accessories

Choose the weight by feel

  • Reverse pec deck3 × 12–201 left · rest 60–90 s. Open the arms without shrugging.
  • Incline or preacher curl3 × 8–121 left · rest 90 s. Incline with dumbbells, or preacher on the bench or the cable preacher. Upper arm still; lower under control.
  • Pallof press2 × 10–12 per siderest 60 s. Stand side-on to the cable, press straight out and resist turning.
  • Wrist curl2 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Forearms on a bench or the forearm cable machine, palms up; let the handle roll down to your fingers, then curl your wrists up.
  • Reverse wrist curl2 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Straight after the wrist curl, palms down; lift the backs of your hands, then lower slowly.
5

Stretch

6–8 minutes, after the session

  • Lying hamstring strap stretch2 × 45 s per side
  • 90/90 hip switches× 8
  • Figure-4 glute stretch2 × 30 s per side

Heavier80 kg × 5, 5, 5, then paused 2 × 4 @ 75Warm-up 20 × 8 · 35 × 5 · 50 × 3 · 65 × 1

If already on Heavier, add one rep on the first flat set, up to 8, leaving at least two in hand. A missed rep or all-out final set calls for one row less.

Week 8 check. You reported 80 kg for 4–5. Six clean reps at 80 kg at comparable effort would be a rep best. Record the first set on the row you earned, its RPE and the rest of the work. An extra max set is unnecessary.

Lighter125 kg × 5, 5, 5Warm-up 60 × 5 · 85 × 3 · 105 × 1
Heavier135 kg × 5, 5, 5Warm-up 60 × 5 · 65 × 5 · 90 × 3 · 115 × 1

Work range 3 × 4–5. Use the row your log supports. Start a new weight at the bottom of the rep range. Move up 5 kg on a future main day only after two comparable sessions at the same weight reach the top on every set, with at least two clean reps left on the final set both times. With one left, repeat. After a miss or a grind, use the next lighter load. Easier weeks and secondary work do not count.

Accessories

Choose the weight by feel

Thursday 24 December · Week 8 · Upper B

Pull-ups, strict push-ups and incline bench

About 55 min

1

Pull-ups

Printed reps, then one set to 1 left

  1. Set 116reps
  2. Set 213reps
  3. Set 3Max, stop with 1 left

Rest 3 min between sets. If you reach 1 rep left before a printed number, stop there.

2

Strict push-ups

Printed reps, then one set to 1 left

  1. Set 138reps
  2. Set 2Max, stop with 1 left

Rest 3 min between sets. If you reach 1 rep left before a printed number, stop there.

3

Low-incline bench press

Two work sets · 2 clean reps left

Use a 30° bench. Warm up with 20 kg × 8 for working weights of 25 kg or more. Add 30 kg × 5 when working at 35 kg or above.

  1. Set 18–12reps
  2. Set 28–12reps

Working weight. First session, try 45 kg total. Use 40 kg if eight clean reps at 45 kg would leave fewer than two in reserve. On later Thursdays, use your last regular working weight. Once both sets reach 12 at the same load with at least two clean reps left, use the next 5 kg step at the next regular session and aim for eight per set.

Barbell steps and setup

20 · 25 · 30 · 35 · 40 · 45 · 50 · 55 · 60 · 65 · 70 kg. Continue in 5 kg steps. All weights include the bar. For work at 50 kg or above, an extra 1–3 warm-up reps one step below your working weight can help you judge today’s load.

Use safeties or a spotter. Keep your feet planted and your back comfortably supported. Use the same bench setting and controlled range each week. Rest 2–3 minutes between work sets.

If a new weight works for the first eight but is too heavy for the second set, use the previous weight for set two. Log both loads and both rep counts.

4

Accessories

Choose the weight by feel

  • Reverse pec deck3 × 12–201 left · rest 60–90 s. Open the arms without shrugging.
  • Incline or preacher curl3 × 8–121 left · rest 90 s. Incline with dumbbells, or preacher on the bench or the cable preacher. Upper arm still; lower under control.
  • Pallof press2 × 10–12 per siderest 60 s. Stand side-on to the cable, press straight out and resist turning.
  • Wrist curl2 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Forearms on a bench or the forearm cable machine, palms up; let the handle roll down to your fingers, then curl your wrists up.
  • Reverse wrist curl2 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Straight after the wrist curl, palms down; lift the backs of your hands, then lower slowly.
5

Stretch

6–8 minutes, after the session

  • Lying hamstring strap stretch2 × 45 s per side
  • 90/90 hip switches× 8
  • Figure-4 glute stretch2 × 30 s per side

  • Goblet squat hold at your depth2 × 20–30 s
  • Hip-hinge drill× 8
2

Sumo deadlift

Main session

Warm-up in kg　60 × 5 · 65 × 5 · 90 × 3 · 115 × 1

  1. Set 1135kg×4
  2. Set 2135kg×4
  3. Set 3135kg×4

Start each rep from a full reset. Brace, keep the bar close and use your usual lockout. Rest 3–5 minutes.

Lighter130 kg × 4, 4, 4Warm-up 60 × 5 · 90 × 3 · 110 × 1
Heavier140 kg × 4, 4, 4Warm-up 60 × 5 · 65 × 5 · 95 × 3 · 120 × 1

Work range 3 × 3–4. Use the row your log supports. Start a new weight at the bottom of the rep range. Move up 5 kg on a future main day only after two comparable sessions at the same weight reach the top on every set, with at least two clean reps left on the final set both times. With one left, repeat. After a miss or a grind, use the next lighter load. Easier weeks and secondary work do not count.

All load options and warm-ups
  • 130 kg for 3 × 3–4. Warm-up in kg: 60 × 5 · 90 × 3 · 110 × 1.
  • 135 kg for 3 × 3–4. Warm-up in kg: 60 × 5 · 65 × 5 · 90 × 3 · 115 × 1.
  • 140 kg for 3 × 3–4. Warm-up in kg: 60 × 5 · 65 × 5 · 95 × 3 · 120 × 1.
  • 145 kg for 3 × 3–4. Warm-up in kg: 60 × 5 · 70 × 5 · 100 × 3 · 120 × 1.
  • 150 kg for 3 × 3–4. Warm-up in kg: 60 × 5 · 70 × 5 · 100 × 3 · 125 × 1.
3

Accessories

Choose the weight by feel

  • Bulgarian split squat3 × 8–12 per leg2 left · rest 2 min. Rear foot on a bench, dumbbells at your sides; straps if grip fades.
  • Leg press3 × 10–151–2 left · rest 2 min. Lower until just before your pelvis starts to roll off the pad. If it's taken, use the hack squat for the same reps, with your heels down and your lower back on the pad.
  • Seated calf raise machine3 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Pause 1–2 s in the bottom stretch; no bouncing.
  • Hip abduction machine3 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Sit tall, push your knees out, pause 1 s with your legs wide, then return slowly.
  • Hip adduction machine2 × 10–151–2 left · rest 60–90 s. Start as wide as is comfortable, squeeze the pads together, then open slowly without bouncing.
4

Stretch

6–8 minutes, after the session

  • Lying hamstring strap stretch2 × 45 s per side
  • Half-kneeling hip flexor2 × 30 s per side
  • Frog inner-thigh stretch2 × 45 s
  • Wall calf stretch2 × 30 s per side

Thursday 7 January · Week 10 · Upper B

Pull-ups, strict push-ups and incline bench

About 55 min

1

Pull-ups

Printed reps, then one set to 1 left

  1. Set 116reps
  2. Set 213reps
  3. Set 3Max, stop with 1 left

Rest 3 min between sets. If you reach 1 rep left before a printed number, stop there.

2

Strict push-ups

Printed reps, then one set to 1 left

  1. Set 138reps
  2. Set 2Max, stop with 1 left

Rest 3 min between sets. If you reach 1 rep left before a printed number, stop there.

3

Low-incline bench press

Two work sets · 2 clean reps left

Use a 30° bench. Warm up with 20 kg × 8 for working weights of 25 kg or more. Add 30 kg × 5 when working at 35 kg or above.

  1. Set 18–12reps
  2. Set 28–12reps

Working weight. First session, try 45 kg total. Use 40 kg if eight clean reps at 45 kg would leave fewer than two in reserve. On later Thursdays, use your last regular working weight. Once both sets reach 12 at the same load with at least two clean reps left, use the next 5 kg step at the next regular session and aim for eight per set. Carry your log forward from the training Thursday before the benchmark week.

Barbell steps and setup

20 · 25 · 30 · 35 · 40 · 45 · 50 · 55 · 60 · 65 · 70 kg. Continue in 5 kg steps. All weights include the bar. For work at 50 kg or above, an extra 1–3 warm-up reps one step below your working weight can help you judge today’s load.

Use safeties or a spotter. Keep your feet planted and your back comfortably supported. Use the same bench setting and controlled range each week. Rest 2–3 minutes between work sets.

If a new weight works for the first eight but is too heavy for the second set, use the previous weight for set two. Log both loads and both rep counts.

4

Accessories

Choose the weight by feel

  • Reverse pec deck3 × 12–201 left · rest 60–90 s. Open the arms without shrugging.
  • Incline or preacher curl3 × 8–121 left · rest 90 s. Incline with dumbbells, or preacher on the bench or the cable preacher. Upper arm still; lower under control.
  • Pallof press2 × 10–12 per siderest 60 s. Stand side-on to the cable, press straight out and resist turning.
  • Wrist curl2 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Forearms on a bench or the forearm cable machine, palms up; let the handle roll down to your fingers, then curl your wrists up.
  • Reverse wrist curl2 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Straight after the wrist curl, palms down; lift the backs of your hands, then lower slowly.
5

Stretch

6–8 minutes, after the session

  • Lying hamstring strap stretch2 × 45 s per side
  • 90/90 hip switches× 8
  • Figure-4 glute stretch2 × 30 s per side

Tuesday 19 January · Week 12 · Upper A

Heavy bench

About 65 min

1

Bench press

Practice single, then flat and paused work

Warm-up in kg　20 × 8 · 35 × 5 · 55 × 3 · 65 × 2 · 75 × 1 · 80 × 1

  1. Practice single · RPE 785kg×1
  2. Set 180kg×4
  3. Set 280kg×3
  4. Set 380kg×3
  5. Then paused bench
  6. Paused 175kg×3
  7. Paused 275kg×3

Rest 3–5 min. Touch the chest with no bounce; safeties set or a spotter. Paused sets: 1-second pause on the chest, rest 3 min.

LighterPractice single 80 kg × 1, then 75 kg × 4, 3, 3, then paused 2 × 3 @ 70Warm-up 20 × 8 · 35 × 5 · 50 × 3 · 60 × 2 · 70 × 1 · 75 × 1
HeavierPractice single 90 kg × 1, then 85 kg × 4, 3, 3, then paused 2 × 3 @ 80Warm-up 20 × 8 · 40 × 5 · 55 × 3 · 70 × 2 · 75 × 1 · 85 × 1

Choose next week’s row. After two regular Tuesdays in the same row with all flat and paused reps complete and three or more left on the last unpaused set, move up one row next regular Tuesday. If already on Heavier, add one rep on the first flat set, up to 6, leaving at least two in hand. A missed rep or all-out final set calls for one row less.

Practice single. Take one smooth practice single around RPE 7 (about three clean reps in reserve) before the work sets. Select the single independently from the work weight using your log. If a warm-up already reaches that effort, count it and end the ramp. This single does not qualify you for a load increase. Suggested loads are 80 / 85 / 90 kg.

2

Weighted pull-up

Main session

Warm-up  BW × 5 · +7.5 × 3

  1. Set 1+17.5kg×4
  2. Set 2+17.5kg×4
  3. Set 3+17.5kg×4
  4. Set 4+17.5kg×4

Heavier+20 kg × 4, 4, 4, 4Warm-up BW × 5 · +10 × 3

Accessories

Wednesday 20 January · Week 12 · Lower B

Sumo

About 65 min

1

Mobility warm-up

Before the first lift

  • Easy bike or walk3 min
  • Knee-to-wall ankle rocks2 × 10 per side
  • Adductor rock-backs× 10
  • Goblet squat hold at your depth2 × 20–30 s
  • Hip-hinge drill× 8
2

Sumo deadlift

Practice single and work sets

Warm-up in kg　60 × 5 · 65 × 5 · 95 × 3 · 115 × 2 · 130 × 1 · 140 × 1

  1. Practice single · RPE 7150kg×1
  2. Set 1140kg×3
  3. Set 2140kg×3
  4. Set 3140kg×3

Practice single. Take one smooth practice single around RPE 7 (about three clean reps in reserve) before the work sets. Select the single independently from the work weight using your log. If a warm-up already reaches that effort, count it and end the ramp. This single does not qualify you for a load increase.

Start each rep from a full reset. Brace, keep the bar close and use your usual lockout. Rest 3–5 minutes.

LighterPractice single 145 kg × 1 → 135 kg × 3, 3, 3Warm-up 60 × 5 · 90 × 3 · 110 × 2 · 125 × 1 · 135 × 1
HeavierPractice single 155 kg × 1 → 145 kg × 3, 3, 3Warm-up 60 × 5 · 65 × 5 · 95 × 3 · 120 × 2 · 135 × 1 · 145 × 1

Work range 3 × 3–4. Use the row your log supports. Start a new weight at the bottom of the rep range. Move up 5 kg on a future main day only after two comparable sessions at the same weight reach the top on every set, with at least two clean reps left on the final set both times. With one left, repeat. After a miss or a grind, use the next lighter load. Easier weeks and secondary work do not count.

Choose the single separately. 145 / 150 / 155 kg are examples. Use a lighter warm-up when it already reaches RPE 7.

All load options and warm-ups
  • 130 kg for 3 × 3–4. Warm-up in kg: 60 × 5 · 90 × 3 · 110 × 1.
  • 135 kg for 3 × 3–4. Warm-up in kg: 60 × 5 · 65 × 5 · 90 × 3 · 115 × 1.
  • 140 kg for 3 × 3–4. Warm-up in kg: 60 × 5 · 65 × 5 · 95 × 3 · 120 × 1.
  • 145 kg for 3 × 3–4. Warm-up in kg: 60 × 5 · 70 × 5 · 100 × 3 · 120 × 1.
  • 150 kg for 3 × 3–4. Warm-up in kg: 60 × 5 · 70 × 5 · 100 × 3 · 125 × 1.
3

Accessories

Choose the weight by feel

  • Bulgarian split squat3 × 8–12 per leg2 left · rest 2 min. Rear foot on a bench, dumbbells at your sides; straps if grip fades.
  • Leg press3 × 10–151–2 left · rest 2 min. Lower until just before your pelvis starts to roll off the pad. If it's taken, use the hack squat for the same reps, with your heels down and your lower back on the pad.
  • Seated calf raise machine3 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Pause 1–2 s in the bottom stretch; no bouncing.
  • Hip abduction machine3 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Sit tall, push your knees out, pause 1 s with your legs wide, then return slowly.
  • Hip adduction machine2 × 10–151–2 left · rest 60–90 s. Start as wide as is comfortable, squeeze the pads together, then open slowly without bouncing.
4

Stretch

6–8 minutes, after the session

  • Lying hamstring strap stretch2 × 45 s per side
  • Half-kneeling hip flexor2 × 30 s per side
  • Frog inner-thigh stretch2 × 45 s
  • Wall calf stretch2 × 30 s per side

Thursday 21 January · Week 12 · Upper B

Pull-ups, strict push-ups and incline bench

About 55 min

1

Pull-ups

Printed reps, then one set to 1 left

  1. Set 117reps
  2. Set 214reps
  3. Set 3Max, stop with 1 left

Rest 3 min between sets. If you reach 1 rep left before a printed number, stop there.

2

Strict push-ups

Printed reps, then one set to 1 left

  1. Set 139reps
  2. Set 2Max, stop with 1 left

Rest 3 min between sets. If you reach 1 rep left before a printed number, stop there.

3

Low-incline bench press

Two work sets · 2 clean reps left

Use a 30° bench. Warm up with 20 kg × 8 for working weights of 25 kg or more. Add 30 kg × 5 when working at 35 kg or above.

  1. Set 18–12reps
  2. Set 28–12reps

Working weight. First session, try 45 kg total. Use 40 kg if eight clean reps at 45 kg would leave fewer than two in reserve. On later Thursdays, use your last regular working weight. Once both sets reach 12 at the same load with at least two clean reps left, use the next 5 kg step at the next regular session and aim for eight per set.

Barbell steps and setup

20 · 25 · 30 · 35 · 40 · 45 · 50 · 55 · 60 · 65 · 70 kg. Continue in 5 kg steps. All weights include the bar. For work at 50 kg or above, an extra 1–3 warm-up reps one step below your working weight can help you judge today’s load.

Use safeties or a spotter. Keep your feet planted and your back comfortably supported. Use the same bench setting and controlled range each week. Rest 2–3 minutes between work sets.

If a new weight works for the first eight but is too heavy for the second set, use the previous weight for set two. Log both loads and both rep counts.

4

Accessories

Choose the weight by feel

  • Reverse pec deck3 × 12–201 left · rest 60–90 s. Open the arms without shrugging.
  • Incline or preacher curl3 × 8–121 left · rest 90 s. Incline with dumbbells, or preacher on the bench or the cable preacher. Upper arm still; lower under control.
  • Pallof press2 × 10–12 per siderest 60 s. Stand side-on to the cable, press straight out and resist turning.
  • Wrist curl2 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Forearms on a bench or the forearm cable machine, palms up; let the handle roll down to your fingers, then curl your wrists up.
  • Reverse wrist curl2 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Straight after the wrist curl, palms down; lift the backs of your hands, then lower slowly.
5

Stretch

6–8 minutes, after the session

  • Lying hamstring strap stretch2 × 45 s per side
  • 90/90 hip switches× 8
  • Figure-4 glute stretch2 × 30 s per side

Back squat

Main session

Warm-up in kg　20 × 8 · 55 × 5 · 80 × 3 · 95 × 1

  1. Set 1115kg×4
  2. Set 2115kg×4
  3. Set 3115kg×4
  4. Set 4115kg×4

Use the depth you recorded in week 1. Brace before each rep and use rack safeties. Rest 3–5 minutes.

Lighter110 kg × 4, 4, 4, 4Warm-up 20 × 8 · 55 × 5 · 75 × 3 · 90 × 1
Heavier120 kg × 4, 4, 4, 4Warm-up 20 × 8 · 60 × 5 · 80 × 3 · 100 × 1

Work range 4 × 3–4. Use the row your log supports. Start a new weight at the bottom of the rep range. Move up 5 kg on a future main day only after two comparable sessions at the same weight reach the top on every set, with at least two clean reps left on the final set both times. With one left, repeat. After a miss or a grind, use the next lighter load. Easier weeks and secondary work do not count.

All load options and warm-ups
  • 105 kg for 4 × 3–4. Warm-up in kg: 20 × 8 · 50 × 5 · 70 × 3 · 90 × 1.

Heavier120 kg × 3, 3Warm-up 60 × 5 · 95 × 2

Lighter75 kg × 4, 4, 3, then paused 2 × 3 @ 70Warm-up 20 × 8 · 35 × 5 · 50 × 3 · 60 × 1
Heavier85 kg × 4, 4, 3, then paused 2 × 3 @ 80Warm-up 20 × 8 · 40 × 5 · 55 × 3 · 70 × 1

Choose next week’s row. After two regular Tuesdays in the same row with all flat and paused reps complete and three or more left on the last unpaused set, move up one row next regular Tuesday. If already on Heavier, add one rep on the first flat set, up to 6, leaving at least two in hand. A missed rep or all-out final set calls for one row less.

2

Weighted pull-up

Main session

Warm-up  BW × 5 · +7.5 × 3

  1. Set 1+17.5kg×5
  2. Set 2+17.5kg×4
  3. Set 3+17.5kg×4
  4. Set 4+17.5kg×4

Lighter+15 kg × 5, 4, 4, 4Warm-up BW × 5 · +7.5 × 3
Heavier+20 kg × 5, 4, 4, 4Warm-up BW × 5 · +10 × 3
3

Accessories

Choose the weight by feel

  • Chest-supported dumbbell row3 × 8–121–2 left · rest 2 min. Chest on an incline bench; pull the elbows toward your hips. A row machine with a chest pad works too.
  • Cable lateral raise3 × 12–201 left · rest 60–90 s. Raise to shoulder height without swinging. The standing multi-flight machine works just as well.
  • Hanging knee raise2 × 8–151–2 left · rest 90 s. Curl the pelvis up slightly at the top; no swinging.

Lighter135 kg × 4, 4, 4Warm-up 60 × 5 · 65 × 5 · 90 × 3 · 115 × 1
Heavier145 kg × 4, 4, 4Warm-up 60 × 5 · 70 × 5 · 100 × 3 · 120 × 1

Work range 3 × 3–4. Use the row your log supports. Start a new weight at the bottom of the rep range. Move up 5 kg on a future main day only after two comparable sessions at the same weight reach the top on every set, with at least two clean reps left on the final set both times. With one left, repeat. After a miss or a grind, use the next lighter load. Easier weeks and secondary work do not count.

All load options and warm-ups
  • 130 kg for 3 × 3–4. Warm-up in kg: 60 × 5 · 90 × 3 · 110 × 1.
  • 135 kg for 3 × 3–4. Warm-up in kg: 60 × 5 · 65 × 5 · 90 × 3 · 115 × 1.
  • 140 kg for 3 × 3–4. Warm-up in kg: 60 × 5 · 65 × 5 · 95 × 3 · 120 × 1.
  • 145 kg for 3 × 3–4. Warm-up in kg: 60 × 5 · 70 × 5 · 100 × 3 · 120 × 1.
  • 150 kg for 3 × 3–4. Warm-up in kg: 60 × 5 · 70 × 5 · 100 × 3 · 125 × 1.
3

Accessories

Choose the weight by feel

  • Bulgarian split squat3 × 8–12 per leg2 left · rest 2 min. Rear foot on a bench, dumbbells at your sides; straps if grip fades.
  • Leg press3 × 10–151–2 left · rest 2 min. Lower until just before your pelvis starts to roll off the pad. If it's taken, use the hack squat for the same reps, with your heels down and your lower back on the pad.
  • Seated calf raise machine3 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Pause 1–2 s in the bottom stretch; no bouncing.
  • Hip abduction machine3 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Sit tall, push your knees out, pause 1 s with your legs wide, then return slowly.
  • Hip adduction machine2 × 10–151–2 left · rest 60–90 s. Start as wide as is comfortable, squeeze the pads together, then open slowly without bouncing.
4

Stretch

6–8 minutes, after the session

  • Lying hamstring strap stretch2 × 45 s per side
  • Half-kneeling hip flexor2 × 30 s per side
  • Frog inner-thigh stretch2 × 45 s
  • Wall calf stretch2 × 30 s per side

Thursday 4 February · Week 14 · Upper B

Pull-ups, strict push-ups and incline bench

About 55 min

1

Pull-ups

Printed reps, then one set to 1 left

  1. Set 117reps
  2. Set 214reps
  3. Set 3Max, stop with 1 left

Rest 3 min between sets. If you reach 1 rep left before a printed number, stop there.

2

Strict push-ups

Printed reps, then one set to 1 left

  1. Set 140reps
  2. Set 2Max, stop with 1 left

Rest 3 min between sets. If you reach 1 rep left before a printed number, stop there.

3

Low-incline bench press

Two work sets · 2 clean reps left

Use a 30° bench. Warm up with 20 kg × 8 for working weights of 25 kg or more. Add 30 kg × 5 when working at 35 kg or above.

  1. Set 18–12reps
  2. Set 28–12reps

Working weight. First session, try 45 kg total. Use 40 kg if eight clean reps at 45 kg would leave fewer than two in reserve. On later Thursdays, use your last regular working weight. Once both sets reach 12 at the same load with at least two clean reps left, use the next 5 kg step at the next regular session and aim for eight per set.

Barbell steps and setup

20 · 25 · 30 · 35 · 40 · 45 · 50 · 55 · 60 · 65 · 70 kg. Continue in 5 kg steps. All weights include the bar. For work at 50 kg or above, an extra 1–3 warm-up reps one step below your working weight can help you judge today’s load.

Use safeties or a spotter. Keep your feet planted and your back comfortably supported. Use the same bench setting and controlled range each week. Rest 2–3 minutes between work sets.

If a new weight works for the first eight but is too heavy for the second set, use the previous weight for set two. Log both loads and both rep counts.

4

Accessories

Choose the weight by feel

  • Reverse pec deck3 × 12–201 left · rest 60–90 s. Open the arms without shrugging.
  • Incline or preacher curl3 × 8–121 left · rest 90 s. Incline with dumbbells, or preacher on the bench or the cable preacher. Upper arm still; lower under control.
  • Pallof press2 × 10–12 per siderest 60 s. Stand side-on to the cable, press straight out and resist turning.
  • Wrist curl2 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Forearms on a bench or the forearm cable machine, palms up; let the handle roll down to your fingers, then curl your wrists up.
  • Reverse wrist curl2 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Straight after the wrist curl, palms down; lift the backs of your hands, then lower slowly.
5

Stretch

6–8 minutes, after the session

  • Lying hamstring strap stretch2 × 45 s per side
  • 90/90 hip switches× 8
  • Figure-4 glute stretch2 × 30 s per side

Back squat

Practice single and work sets

Warm-up in kg　20 × 8 · 55 × 5 · 80 × 3 · 95 × 2 · 110 × 1 · 120 × 1

  1. Practice single · RPE 7125kg×1
  2. Set 1120kg×4
  3. Set 2120kg×4
  4. Set 3120kg×4
  5. Set 4120kg×4

Practice single. Take one smooth practice single around RPE 7 (about three clean reps in reserve) before the work sets. Select the single independently from the work weight using your log. If a warm-up already reaches that effort, count it and end the ramp. This single does not qualify you for a load increase.

Use the depth you recorded in week 1. Brace before each rep and use rack safeties. Rest 3–5 minutes.

LighterPractice single 120 kg × 1 → 115 kg × 4, 4, 4, 4Warm-up 20 × 8 · 50 × 5 · 75 × 3 · 90 × 2 · 105 × 1 · 115 × 1
HeavierPractice single 130 kg × 1 → 125 kg × 4, 4, 4, 4Warm-up 20 × 8 · 55 × 5 · 80 × 3 · 100 × 2 · 110 × 1 · 120 × 1

Work range 4 × 3–4. Use the row your log supports. Start a new weight at the bottom of the rep range. Move up 5 kg on a future main day only after two comparable sessions at the same weight reach the top on every set, with at least two clean reps left on the final set both times. With one left, repeat. After a miss or a grind, use the next lighter load. Easier weeks and secondary work do not count.

Choose the single separately. 120 / 125 / 130 kg are examples. Use a lighter warm-up when it already reaches RPE 7.

LighterPractice single 85 kg × 1, then 75 kg × 4, 4, 4, then paused 2 × 3 @ 70Warm-up 20 × 8 · 35 × 5 · 55 × 3 · 65 × 2 · 75 × 1 · 80 × 1
HeavierPractice single 95 kg × 1, then 85 kg × 4, 4, 4, then paused 2 × 3 @ 80Warm-up 20 × 8 · 40 × 5 · 60 × 3 · 70 × 2 · 80 × 1 · 90 × 1

Choose next week’s row. After two regular Tuesdays in the same row with all flat and paused reps complete and three or more left on the last unpaused set, move up one row next regular Tuesday. If already on Heavier, add one rep on the first flat set, up to 6, leaving at least two in hand. A missed rep or all-out final set calls for one row less.

Week 16 check. The Normal row reaches 80 kg for 4, 4, 4. Heavier reaches 85 kg for 4, 4, 4. Compare all three sets and your final-set effort with week 10. Use the row you earned.

Thursday 18 February · Week 16 · Upper B

Pull-ups, strict push-ups and incline bench

About 55 min

1

Pull-ups

Printed reps, then one set to 1 left

  1. Set 118reps
  2. Set 215reps
  3. Set 3Max, stop with 1 left

Rest 3 min between sets. If you reach 1 rep left before a printed number, stop there.

2

Strict push-ups

Printed reps, then one set to 1 left

  1. Set 141reps
  2. Set 2Max, stop with 1 left

Rest 3 min between sets. If you reach 1 rep left before a printed number, stop there.

3

Low-incline bench press

Two work sets · 2 clean reps left

Use a 30° bench. Warm up with 20 kg × 8 for working weights of 25 kg or more. Add 30 kg × 5 when working at 35 kg or above.

  1. Set 18–12reps
  2. Set 28–12reps

Working weight. First session, try 45 kg total. Use 40 kg if eight clean reps at 45 kg would leave fewer than two in reserve. On later Thursdays, use your last regular working weight. Once both sets reach 12 at the same load with at least two clean reps left, use the next 5 kg step at the next regular session and aim for eight per set.

Barbell steps and setup

20 · 25 · 30 · 35 · 40 · 45 · 50 · 55 · 60 · 65 · 70 kg. Continue in 5 kg steps. All weights include the bar. For work at 50 kg or above, an extra 1–3 warm-up reps one step below your working weight can help you judge today’s load.

Use safeties or a spotter. Keep your feet planted and your back comfortably supported. Use the same bench setting and controlled range each week. Rest 2–3 minutes between work sets.

If a new weight works for the first eight but is too heavy for the second set, use the previous weight for set two. Log both loads and both rep counts.

4

Accessories

Choose the weight by feel

  • Reverse pec deck3 × 12–201 left · rest 60–90 s. Open the arms without shrugging.
  • Incline or preacher curl3 × 8–121 left · rest 90 s. Incline with dumbbells, or preacher on the bench or the cable preacher. Upper arm still; lower under control.
  • Pallof press2 × 10–12 per siderest 60 s. Stand side-on to the cable, press straight out and resist turning.
  • Wrist curl2 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Forearms on a bench or the forearm cable machine, palms up; let the handle roll down to your fingers, then curl your wrists up.
  • Reverse wrist curl2 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Straight after the wrist curl, palms down; lift the backs of your hands, then lower slowly.
5

Stretch

6–8 minutes, after the session

  • Lying hamstring strap stretch2 × 45 s per side
  • 90/90 hip switches× 8
  • Figure-4 glute stretch2 × 30 s per side

Lighter120 kg × 1, then 105 kg × 3, 3, 3Warm-up 20 × 8 · 50 × 5 · 75 × 3 · 90 × 2 · 105 × 1 · 115 × 1
Heavier130 kg × 1, then 115 kg × 3, 3, 3Warm-up 20 × 8 · 55 × 5 · 80 × 3 · 100 × 2 · 110 × 1 · 120 × 1

Perform one working single. Earn the next 5 kg after two comparable main sessions at the same weight on different weeks: single at RPE 7.5 or easier, all back-off reps complete, and at least two clean reps left on the last set both times. Repeat at RPE 8–8.5. At RPE 9 or above, reduce the next heavy-session load by 5 kg. Choose a lighter single today if warm-ups feel unusually heavy.

Thursday 4 March · Week 18 · Upper B

Pull-ups, strict push-ups and incline bench

About 55 min

1

Pull-ups

Printed reps, then one set to 1 left

  1. Set 118reps
  2. Set 215reps
  3. Set 3Max, stop with 1 left

Rest 3 min between sets. If you reach 1 rep left before a printed number, stop there.

2

Strict push-ups

Printed reps, then one set to 1 left

  1. Set 142reps
  2. Set 2Max, stop with 1 left

Rest 3 min between sets. If you reach 1 rep left before a printed number, stop there.

3

Low-incline bench press

Two work sets · 2 clean reps left

Use a 30° bench. Warm up with 20 kg × 8 for working weights of 25 kg or more. Add 30 kg × 5 when working at 35 kg or above.

  1. Set 18–12reps
  2. Set 28–12reps

Working weight. First session, try 45 kg total. Use 40 kg if eight clean reps at 45 kg would leave fewer than two in reserve. On later Thursdays, use your last regular working weight. Once both sets reach 12 at the same load with at least two clean reps left, use the next 5 kg step at the next regular session and aim for eight per set. Carry your log forward from the training Thursday before the benchmark week.

Barbell steps and setup

20 · 25 · 30 · 35 · 40 · 45 · 50 · 55 · 60 · 65 · 70 kg. Continue in 5 kg steps. All weights include the bar. For work at 50 kg or above, an extra 1–3 warm-up reps one step below your working weight can help you judge today’s load.

Use safeties or a spotter. Keep your feet planted and your back comfortably supported. Use the same bench setting and controlled range each week. Rest 2–3 minutes between work sets.

If a new weight works for the first eight but is too heavy for the second set, use the previous weight for set two. Log both loads and both rep counts.

4

Accessories

Choose the weight by feel

  • Reverse pec deck3 × 12–201 left · rest 60–90 s. Open the arms without shrugging.
  • Incline or preacher curl3 × 8–121 left · rest 90 s. Incline with dumbbells, or preacher on the bench or the cable preacher. Upper arm still; lower under control.
  • Pallof press2 × 10–12 per siderest 60 s. Stand side-on to the cable, press straight out and resist turning.
  • Wrist curl2 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Forearms on a bench or the forearm cable machine, palms up; let the handle roll down to your fingers, then curl your wrists up.
  • Reverse wrist curl2 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Straight after the wrist curl, palms down; lift the backs of your hands, then lower slowly.
5

Stretch

6–8 minutes, after the session

  • Lying hamstring strap stretch2 × 45 s per side
  • 90/90 hip switches× 8
  • Figure-4 glute stretch2 × 30 s per side

Heavier160 kg × 1, then 140 kg × 3, 3Warm-up 60 × 5 · 70 × 5 · 100 × 3 · 120 × 2 · 140 × 1 · 150 × 1

Perform one working single. Earn the next 5 kg after two comparable main sessions at the same weight on different weeks: single at RPE 7.5 or easier, all back-off reps complete, and at least two clean reps left on the last set both times. Accessories

Choose the weight by feel

  • Seated calf raise machine3 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Pause 1–2 s in the bottom stretch; no bouncing.
  • Hip abduction machine3 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Sit tall, push your knees out, pause 1 s with your legs wide, then return slowly.

Thursday 11 March · Week 19 · Upper B

Pull-ups, strict push-ups and incline bench

About 55 min

2

Strict push-ups

Printed reps, then one set to 1 left

  1. Set 142reps
  2. Set 2Max, stop with 1 left

Rest 3 min between sets. If you reach 1 rep left before a printed number, stop there.

3

Low-incline bench press

Two work sets · 2 clean reps left

Use a 30° bench. Warm up with 20 kg × 8 for working weights of 25 kg or more. Add 30 kg × 5 when working at 35 kg or above.

  1. Set 18–12reps
  2. Set 28–12reps

Working weight. First session, try 45 kg total. Use 40 kg if eight clean reps at 45 kg would leave fewer than two in reserve. On later Thursdays, use your last regular working weight. Once both sets reach 12 at the same load with at least two clean reps left, use the next 5 kg step at the next regular session and aim for eight per set.

Barbell steps and setup

20 · 25 · 30 · 35 · 40 · 45 · 50 · 55 · 60 · 65 · 70 kg. Continue in 5 kg steps. All weights include the bar. For work at 50 kg or above, an extra 1–3 warm-up reps one step below your working weight can help you judge today’s load.

Use safeties or a spotter. Keep your feet planted and your back comfortably supported. Use the same bench setting and controlled range each week. Rest 2–3 minutes between work sets.

If a new weight works for the first eight but is too heavy for the second set, use the previous weight for set two. Log both loads and both rep counts.

4

Accessories

Choose the weight by feel

  • Reverse pec deck3 × 12–201 left · rest 60–90 s. Open the arms without shrugging.
  • Incline or preacher curl3 × 8–121 left · rest 90 s. Incline with dumbbells, or preacher on the bench or the cable preacher. Upper arm still; lower under control.
  • Pallof press2 × 10–12 per siderest 60 s. Stand side-on to the cable, press straight out and resist turning.
  • Wrist curl2 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Forearms on a bench or the forearm cable machine, palms up; let the handle roll down to your fingers, then curl your wrists up.
  • Reverse wrist curl2 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Straight after the wrist curl, palms down; lift the backs of your hands, then lower slowly.
5

6–8 minutes, after the session

  • Lying hamstring strap stretch2 × 45 s per side

  • Figure-4 glute stretch2 × 30 s per side

Repeat at RPE 8–8.5. At RPE 9 or above, reduce the next heavy-session load by 5 kg. Choose a lighter single today if warm-ups feel unusually heavy.

Thursday 25 March · Week 21 · Upper B

Pull-ups, strict push-ups and incline bench

About 55 min

1

Pull-ups

Printed reps, then one set to 1 left

  1. Set 119reps
  2. Set 216reps
  3. Set 3Max, stop with 1 left

Rest 3 min between sets. If you reach 1 rep left before a printed number, stop there.

2

Strict push-ups

Printed reps, then one set to 1 left

  1. Set 143reps
  2. Set 2Max, stop with 1 left

Rest 3 min between sets. If you reach 1 rep left before a printed number, stop there.

3

Low-incline bench press

Two work sets · 2 clean reps left

Use a 30° bench. Warm up with 20 kg × 8 for working weights of 25 kg or more. Add 30 kg × 5 when working at 35 kg or above.

  1. Set 18–12reps
  2. Set 28–12reps

Working weight. First session, try 45 kg total. Use 40 kg if eight clean reps at 45 kg would leave fewer than two in reserve. On later Thursdays, use your last regular working weight. Once both sets reach 12 at the same load with at least two clean reps left, use the next 5 kg step at the next regular session and aim for eight per set.

Barbell steps and setup

20 · 25 · 30 · 35 · 40 · 45 · 50 · 55 · 60 · 65 · 70 kg. Continue in 5 kg steps. All weights include the bar. For work at 50 kg or above, an extra 1–3 warm-up reps one step below your working weight can help you judge today’s load.

Use safeties or a spotter. Keep your feet planted and your back comfortably supported. Use the same bench setting and controlled range each week. Rest 2–3 minutes between work sets.

If a new weight works for the first eight but is too heavy for the second set, use the previous weight for set two. Log both loads and both rep counts.

4

Accessories

Choose the weight by feel

  • Reverse pec deck3 × 12–201 left · rest 60–90 s. Open the arms without shrugging.
  • Incline or preacher curl3 × 8–121 left · rest 90 s. Incline with dumbbells, or preacher on the bench or the cable preacher. Upper arm still; lower under control.
  • Pallof press2 × 10–12 per siderest 60 s. Stand side-on to the cable, press straight out and resist turning.
  • Wrist curl2 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Forearms on a bench or the forearm cable machine, palms up; let the handle roll down to your fingers, then curl your wrists up.
  • Reverse wrist curl2 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Straight after the wrist curl, palms down; lift the backs of your hands, then lower slowly.
5

Stretch

6–8 minutes, after the session

  • Lying hamstring strap stretch2 × 45 s per side
  • 90/90 hip switches× 8
  • Figure-4 glute stretch2 × 30 s per side

20 · 25 · 30 · 35 · 40 · 45 · 50 · 55 · 60 · 65 · 70 kg. Continue in 5 kg steps. All weights include the bar. For work at 50 kg or above, an extra 1–3 warm-up reps one step below your working weight can help you judge today’s load.

Use safeties or a spotter. Keep your feet planted and your back comfortably supported. Use the same bench setting and controlled range each week. Rest 2–3 minutes between work sets.

If a new weight works for the first eight but is too heavy for the second set, use the previous weight for set two. Log both loads and both rep counts.

4

Accessories

Choose the weight by feel

  • Reverse pec deck3 × 12–201 left · rest 60–90 s. Open the arms without shrugging.
  • Incline or preacher curl3 × 8–121 left · rest 90 s. Incline with dumbbells, or preacher on the bench or the cable preacher. Upper arm still; lower under control.
  • Pallof press2 × 10–12 per siderest 60 s. Stand side-on to the cable, press straight out and resist turning.
  • Wrist curl2 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Forearms on a bench or the forearm cable machine, palms up; let the handle roll down to your fingers, then curl your wrists up.
  • Reverse wrist curl2 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Straight after the wrist curl, palms down; lift the backs of your hands, then lower slowly.
5

6–8 minutes, after the session

  • Lying hamstring strap stretch2 × 45 s per side
  • 90/90 hip switches× 8
  • Figure-4 glute stretch2 × 30 s per side

Lighter130 kg × 1, then 115 kg × 2, 2, 2Warm-up 20 × 8 · 55 × 5 · 80 × 3 · 100 × 2 · 110 × 1 · 120 × 1
Heavier140 kg × 1, then 125 kg × 2, 2, 2Warm-up 20 × 8 · 60 × 5 · 85 × 3 · 105 × 2 · 120 × 1 · 130 × 1

Perform one working single. Earn the next 5 kg after two comparable main sessions at the same weight on different weeks: single at RPE 7.5 or easier, all back-off reps complete, and at least two clean reps left on the last set both times. Repeat at RPE 8–8.5. At RPE 9 or above, reduce the next heavy-session load by 5 kg. Choose a lighter single today if warm-ups feel unusually heavy.

Pull-ups, strict push-ups and incline bench

About 55 min

1

Pull-ups

Printed reps, then one set to 1 left

  1. Set 119reps
  2. Set 216reps
  3. Set 3Max, stop with 1 left

Rest 3 min between sets. If you reach 1 rep left before a printed number, stop there.

2

Strict push-ups

Printed reps, then one set to 1 left

  1. Set 144reps
  2. Set 2Max, stop with 1 left

Rest 3 min between sets. If you reach 1 rep left before a printed number, stop there.

3

Low-incline bench press

Two work sets · 2 clean reps left

Use a 30° bench. Warm up with 20 kg × 8 for working weights of 25 kg or more. Add 30 kg × 5 when working at 35 kg or above.

  1. Set 18–12reps
  2. Set 28–12reps

Working weight. First session, try 45 kg total. Use 40 kg if eight clean reps at 45 kg would leave fewer than two in reserve. On later Thursdays, use your last regular working weight. Once both sets reach 12 at the same load with at least two clean reps left, use the next 5 kg step at the next regular session and aim for eight per set.

Barbell steps and setup

20 · 25 · 30 · 35 · 40 · 45 · 50 · 55 · 60 · 65 · 70 kg. Continue in 5 kg steps. All weights include the bar. For work at 50 kg or above, an extra 1–3 warm-up reps one step below your working weight can help you judge today’s load.

Use safeties or a spotter. Keep your feet planted and your back comfortably supported. Use the same bench setting and controlled range each week. Rest 2–3 minutes between work sets.

If a new weight works for the first eight but is too heavy for the second set, use the previous weight for set two. Log both loads and both rep counts.

  • Pallof press2 × 10–12 per siderest 60 s. Stand side-on to the cable, press straight out and resist turning.
  • Wrist curl2 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Forearms on a bench or the forearm cable machine, palms up; let the handle roll down to your fingers, then curl your wrists up.
  • Reverse wrist curl2 × 12–201 left · rest 60 s. Straight after the wrist curl, palms down; lift the backs of your hands, then lower slowly.
